deepseek_full_trace_v2 完成 → 若 accuracy > 70%，填跨模型表最后一格 → 更新 paper_framing.md → 启动 Paper Agent (Full Draft)；若仍 collapse，记录 negative + 论文中写 \"DeepSeek full_trace collapsed due to chat template overhead, consistent with information overload hypothesis\"\n- deepseek_exception_only_seed123 完成 → 若 ≈99.3-99.8%，跨模型 seed robustness 确认 → 加入论文表格；若显著低，检查训练日志\n- fig2_calibration Worker 回调 → ## 上一轮交接 (task_context)

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- GPU 白名单：**仅 westd-11235 cuda:0 + cuda:1**，无例外
- DeepSeek 组实验**必须用旧代码**（`load_best_model_at_end` 注释为 `# TEMP_DISABLED`），保持组内一致。13:24 之后 Worker 新增的 L149-152 四行不能用于 DeepSeek 实验
- 同一论文表格的 pairwise CI 必须统一方法（paired 或 unpaired），DeepSeek VOTA/no_trace 缺 `--save_predictions`，论文表格前必须补跑
- 同 GPU 严禁并行 eval+training（实测从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step）
- conda 环境激活路径：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`，不要假设 .venv 存在
- `HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache`（根分区只有 ~30GB）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- **服务器**: westd-11235, 2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8
- **工作目录**: `/root/trace-verdict-study`
- **Python**: `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`（conda base env）
- **模型路径**: Qwen `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct`; DeepSeek `/root/autodl-tmp/models/deepseek-coder-6.7b-instruct/deepseek-ai/deepseek-coder-6___7b-instruct`
- **数据**: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-data/` (4720 train / 1190 test per condition)
- **Checkpoints**: `/root/autodl-tmp/trace-verdict-study-checkpoints/`
- **W&B project**: `trace-verdict-study`
- **活跃进程**: deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v2 RUNNING（cuda:0，PID 未记录在 spec 但有 cron 监控）；deepseek_exception_only_seed123 QUEUED（PID 144507，在 cuda:0 上等 v2 完成）
- **活跃 Worker**: `worker_figure_fig2_calibration` (画 calibration 图) + `worker_eval_cuda1_seed_predictions_crosscheck` (验证 seed predictions)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
刚完成 exception_only seed robustness（3 seeds 全 99.83% ± 0.00）和 DeepSeek 跨模型 4 条件验证（exception_only 0pp gap 确认 universal signal）。正在修复 DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌问题（v2 重训中，chat-template-aware truncation preserving labels）。

**Registry 活跃实验**:
- `deepseek_full_trace_seed42_v2`: **RUNNING** cuda:0 — v5 代码重训，修复 label 截断。预计 ~2h
- `deepseek_exception_only_seed123`: **QUEUED** — 跨模型 seed robustness，等 cuda:0 空出
- `vota_seed456`: **PREPARING** — Director 已降优先级（VOTA 不是 primary claim）

**等待中**:
- 两个 running Worker 回调（fig2 calibration 图 + seed predictions crosscheck）
- deepseek_full_trace_v2 cron 完成回调
- Paper Agent 启动等 framing 更新完成

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- deepseek_full_trace_v2 完成 → 若 accuracy > 70%，填跨模型表最后一格 → 更新 paper_framing.md → 启动 Paper Agent (Full Draft)；若仍 collapse，记录 negative + 论文中写 "DeepSeek full_trace collapsed due to chat template overhead, consistent with information overload hypothesis"
- deepseek_exception_only_seed123 完成 → 若 ≈99.3-99.8%，跨模型 seed robustness 确认 → 加入论文表格；若显著低，检查训练日志
- fig2_calibration Worker 回调 → 审查图表质量，register_artifact
- seed_predictions_crosscheck Worker 回调 → 确认 3 seeds 的 error samples 是否同一组（irreducible samples 假说验证）
- Paper Agent 启动后 → 优先写 Method + Results（数据最充分），Introduction/Discussion 需要 framing 定稿
- 如有 GPU 空闲 + 时间充裕 → plan_005 DebugBench 第二任务验证（增强 generalization claim）

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
**核心发现**: Exception events 是 verdict prediction 的 universal diagnostic signal——跨模型 gap = 0pp（最佳 checkpoint 下 Qwen + DeepSeek 均 99.83%）。论文 narrative 已从 "VOTA trace compression" 转向 "fault indicator isolation"——VOTA 的价值是发现 exception 是充分信号，而非 VOTA 本身的压缩策略。

**决策链**: D013(ae_only Tier 1) → D016(exception universal) → D019(跨模型验证完成, narrative 升级) → D020(ae_only 进入 Claim 3) → D021/D022(full_trace v2 重训)

**易错点**:
- 不要把 DeepSeek epoch1 的 99.83% 和 epoch3 的 99.33% 混用——论文中需要明确说明是哪个 checkpoint，且 DeepSeek 组所有条件都用 epoch3 final 保持一致
- DeepSeek VOTA 84.45% 看着像 negative，但实际是 positive（vs no_trace +11pp），只是跨模型 gap 大——这正好支撑 "exception is universal, VOTA is model-dependent" 的 narrative
- vota_seed123 已 FAILED (OOM on cuda:1)，retry 两次都失败，不要再试——如果真需要 VOTA seed robustness，必须加 --gradient_checkpointing + 确认 GPU 独占
- 投稿 DDL = ARR May 25（10 天），Paper Agent 是今天启动的关键路径

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

_source line 75 · 2026-05-15T18:27:49.921Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01RC5AAuoZnjjtQhxCZi7Neg`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_4/projects/trace-verdict-study/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — trace-verdict-study\\n\\n> v2.1 — Director 审核修正。基于 v2 draft + Reviewer 审查 7 项修复。D019/D020 对齐。\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)\\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nVerdict prediction (pass/fail) is fundamentally a **fault detection** task. The minimal sufficient signal is the **exception event** — a compact, structured diagnostic moment that achieves near-perfect accuracy (Qwen 99.83%, DeepSeek 99.33%, cross-model gap = 0.50pp) regardless of model architecture. In contrast, richer trace representations that require deeper execution reasoning show model-dependent effectiveness: VOTA gap = 14.96pp, no_trace gap = 1.77pp. Information density — not training signal distribution — is the primary bottleneck: even oracle loss masking (label-only) cannot close the 13pp gap between full traces and targeted extraction. The design principle: **extract the highest-specificity diagnostic signal; discard everything else.**\\n\\n## Core Claims (3)\\n\\n### Claim 1: Exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic\\n\\nException summaries alone achieve near-perfect verdict prediction across architecturally distinct models: Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B (99.83%) and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B (99.33%), cross-model gap = 0.50pp (at best checkpoint, gap = 0pp). This contrasts with VOTA (gap = 14.96pp) and no_trace (gap = 1.77pp). Exception events are structurally simple (`[ERROR] type: ... message: ...`) and do not require deep trace reasoning — making them universally extractable.\\n\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: Qwen 99.83%\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: DeepSeek 99.33% (epoch3) / 99.83% (epoch1 best)\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: DeepSeek VOTA 84.45% (gap = 14.96pp)\\n- `deepseek_no_trace_seed42`: DeepSeek no_trace 73.36% (gap = 1.77pp)\\n- `vota_no_ae_seed42`: 99.41% — actual/expected removal 不降，非 trivial string comparison\\n\\n**Seed robustness**: plan_008 运行中（exception_only seed123 + seed456）。\\n\\n### Claim 2: Information density, not gradient dilution, drives trace compression benefit\\n\\nFull traces help verdict prediction (81.8%, +6.6pp over baseline), but VOTA achieves 99.41% (+24.3pp) — a +17.6pp advantage. Label-only loss ablation (plan_002) quantifies the causal decomposition: **gradient dilution contributes ~26% of the gap** (4.5pp; label-only full_trace = 86.2%), **information overload contributes ~74%** (13.0pp; only trace compression resolves this). Even with oracle loss masking that eliminates gradient dilution, full traces still underperform VOTA by 13pp.\\n\\nCross-model evidence reinforces this: VOTA gap (14.96pp) tracks base model execution reasoning capability (Qwen CRUXEval-I 56.5% vs DeepSeek 39.0%, Δ=17.5pp), suggesting trace utilization effectiveness is bounded by pretraining-derived inductive biases. Exception signal bypasses this bottleneck entirely (gap = 0.50pp).\\n\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (gradient dilution fix, +4.5pp over standard full_trace)\\n- `full_trace_seed42_v5`: 81.8% (standard full_trace)\\n- `vota_seed42_v5`: 99.41%\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (VOTA model-dependent)\\n- CRUXEval literature data (Wang et al.)\\n\\n### Claim 3: Two-tier diagnostic hierarchy for verdict prediction\\n\\nComponent ablation reveals verdict-relevant trace information has a strict two-tier hierarchy:\\n- **Tier 1 — diagnostic moment signals**: exception summaries (99.83%) and assertion-point information (99.92%) are individually near-perfect and sufficient for verdict prediction. These signals are structurally simple and model-agnostic (exception cross-model gap = 0.50pp).\\n- **Tier 2 — behavioral patterns**: loop compression (85.6%) carries substantial signal but is model-dependent and fully subsumed by Tier 1.\\n\\nCombining tiers introduces noise rather than helping: exception_only (99.83%) > VOTA full (99.41%), p=0.01. Loop_only ≈ full_trace_label_only (85.6% vs 86.2%) confirms VOTA's loop compression precisely extracts the useful behavioral signal from full traces.\\n\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: 99.83% (Tier 1 exception)\\n- `vota_ae_only_seed42`: 99.92% (Tier 1 assertion-point, 与 exception 统计等价 p=0.80)\\n- `vota_loop_only_seed42`: 85.6% (Tier 2 behavioral)\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (≈ loop_only, confirming extraction equivalence)\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: 99.33% (Tier 1 cross-model validation)\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (Tier 1+2 cross-model: Tier 2 introduces model-dependent degradation)\\n- Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, VOTA ECE=0.0054 (Tier 1 better calibrated)\\n\\n**Design principle**: verdict-optimized traces should extract highest-specificity diagnostic signals; lower-specificity signals are redundant and may introduce noise.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: Existing trace SFT studies (DCSH, TracePile) evaluate only generative tasks and find limited, model-dependent benefit. No prior work has tested trace SFT on verdict tasks (binary pass/fail), nor identified what trace components drive classification performance.\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: DCSH (EMNLP Findings 2025) tested 5 trace formats on generation tasks, finding that \\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others.\\\" TracePile (NeurIPS 2025) scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation. TRACED (ICSE 2024) showed traces help encoder-based classification. All existing trace formats are designed for execution simulation, not fault detection.\\n\\n- **Our framing**: We reframe verdict prediction as **fault detection** and ask: what is the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal? Through systematic component ablation across two architecturally distinct models, we discover that exception events — compact, structured error summaries — are both necessary and sufficient, achieving near-perfect accuracy (99.5%+ avg) with near-zero cross-model gap. Richer trace representations work only when the base model has sufficient execution reasoning capability, making them unreliable for general deployment.\\n\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"Exception-based trace SFT achieves 99.83% verdict accuracy on Qwen and 99.33% on DeepSeek (gap = 0.50pp), while the same models show a 14.96pp gap on VOTA traces — demonstrating that fault detection, not trace reasoning, is the robust mechanism for verdict prediction.\\\"\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Setup**: Trace SFT for code tasks — promising but inconsistent in prior work (DCSH, TracePile). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\\n\\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.1% → full_trace 81.8% → VOTA 99.41%. Label-only loss ablation decomposes the gap: 26% gradient dilution, 74% information overload. Compression improves accuracy through higher information density, not just cost savings.\\n\\n3. **Twist — what component actually drives VOTA?** Component ablation reveals exception_only (99.83%) ≥ full VOTA (99.41%). The \\\"trace reasoning\\\" narrative is wrong — verdict prediction is fault detection. Tier 1 (exceptions + assertion-point) subsumes Tier 2 (loops). Actual/expected removal doesn't hurt (99.41%).\\n\\n4. **Cross-model stress test — the universal vs the contingent**: Exception_only transfers with near-zero gap (0.50pp), but VOTA degrades by 14.96pp on DeepSeek. The model-agnostic component is precisely the one that doesn't require trace reasoning. CRUXEval reasoning gap (17.5pp) parallels VOTA gap — trace utilization is bounded by pretraining.\\n\\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\\n\\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\\n\\n**现状**: DeepSeek full_trace 28.5%, 100% 预测 fail. 根因 (D018): evaluate.py predict() 缺少截断逻辑, 推理时输入远超训练分布导致 mode collapse.\\n\\n**处理方案**: 修复重跑 in progress (D019). Fix Worker 正在修正 evaluate.py 截断逻辑 + 重评 3 个 checkpoint. 如果修复结果在 submission 前就绪, 纳入跨模型对比表 (4/4 conditions). 如果时间不够, 报告为 N/A 并在 Limitations 中透明披露根因.\\n\\n**对论文叙事的影响**: 无. 三个有效条件 (exception_only, VOTA, no_trace) 已足够支撑全部 claims. Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027 vs VOTA ECE=0.0054. 部分样本 loop 正常但有 exception, 引入矛盾信号. |\\n| \\\"CRUXEval gap 与 VOTA gap 的相关性可能是 coincidence\\\" | 明确标注为 hypothesis. 列出 alternatives (tokenizer efficiency, pretraining scale 2.75x, chat template). 不 overclaim. |\\n| \\\"只有 2 个模型\\\" | 同意是 limitation. 两模型在 architecture, pretraining data (5.5T vs 2T), execution reasoning (CRUXEval 17.5pp gap) 上有充分差异, 不是 trivial replication. |\\n| \\\"99.83% 可能 saturated, 看不出 exception 到底学了什么\\\" | Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, ae_only ECE=0.0004. 模型对 pass/fail 的置信度分布有意义差异. |\\n| \\\"只用了 1 个 seed\\\" | plan_008 补充中. 两模型独立达到 99%+ 本身是 implicit 2-seed validation. |\\n| \\\"eval_loss 上升但 accuracy 提升——checkpoint selection 有问题?\\\" | Discussion: token-level loss 与 binary verdict accuracy 解耦. 推荐 verdict-specific metric. The minimal sufficient signal is the **exception event** — a compact, structured diagnostic moment that achieves near-perfect accuracy (Qwen 99.83%, DeepSeek 99.33%, cross-model gap = 0.50pp) regardless of model architecture. In contrast, richer trace representations that require deeper execution reasoning show model-dependent effectiveness: VOTA gap = 14.96pp, no_trace gap = 1.77pp. Information density — not training signal distribution — is the primary bottleneck: even oracle loss masking (label-only) cannot close the 13pp gap between full traces and targeted extraction. The design principle: **extract the highest-specificity diagnostic signal; discard everything else.**\\\\n\\\\n## Core Claims (3)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 1: Exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic\\\\n\\\\nException summaries alone achieve near-perfect verdict prediction across architecturally distinct models: Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B (99.83%) and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B (99.33%), cross-model gap = 0.50pp (at best checkpoint, gap = 0pp). This contrasts with VOTA (gap = 14.96pp) and no_trace (gap = 1.77pp). Exception events are structurally simple (`[ERROR] type: ... message: ...`) and do not require deep trace reasoning — making them universally extractable.\\\\n\\\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: Qwen 99.83%\\\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: DeepSeek 99.33% (epoch3) / 99.83% (epoch1 best)\\\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: DeepSeek VOTA 84.45% (gap = 14.96pp)\\\\n- `deepseek_no_trace_seed42`: DeepSeek no_trace 73.36% (gap = 1.77pp)\\\\n- `vota_no_ae_seed42`: 99.41% — actual/expected removal 不降，非 trivial string comparison\\\\n\\\\n**Seed robustness**: plan_008 运行中（exception_only seed123 + seed456）。\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2: Information density, not gradient dilution, drives trace compression benefit\\\\n\\\\nFull traces help verdict prediction (81.8%, +6.6pp over baseline), but VOTA achieves 99.41% (+24.3pp) — a +17.6pp advantage. Label-only loss ablation (plan_002) quantifies the causal decomposition: **gradient dilution contributes ~26% of the gap** (4.5pp; label-only full_trace = 86.2%), **information overload contributes ~74%** (13.0pp; only trace compression resolves this). Even with oracle loss masking that eliminates gradient dilution, full traces still underperform VOTA by 13pp.\\\\n\\\\nCross-model evidence reinforces this: VOTA gap (14.96pp) tracks base model execution reasoning capability (Qwen CRUXEval-I 56.5% vs DeepSeek 39.0%, Δ=17.5pp), suggesting trace utilization effectiveness is bounded by pretraining-derived inductive biases. Exception signal bypasses this bottleneck entirely (gap = 0.50pp).\\\\n\\\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (gradient dilution fix, +4.5pp over standard full_trace)\\\\n- `full_trace_seed42_v5`: 81.8% (standard full_trace)\\\\n- `vota_seed42_v5`: 99.41%\\\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (VOTA model-dependent)\\\\n- CRUXEval literature data (Wang et al.)\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3: Two-tier diagnostic hierarchy for verdict prediction\\\\n\\\\nComponent ablation reveals verdict-relevant trace information has a strict two-tier hierarchy:\\\\n- **Tier 1 — diagnostic moment signals**: exception summaries (99.83%) and assertion-point information (99.92%) are individually near-perfect and sufficient for verdict prediction. These signals are structurally simple and model-agnostic (exception cross-model gap = 0.50pp).\\\\n- **Tier 2 — behavioral patterns**: loop compression (85.6%) carries substantial signal but is model-dependent and fully subsumed by Tier 1.\\\\n\\\\nCombining tiers introduces noise rather than helping: exception_only (99.83%) > VOTA full (99.41%), p=0.01. Loop_only ≈ full_trace_label_only (85.6% vs 86.2%) confirms VOTA's loop compression precisely extracts the useful behavioral signal from full traces.\\\\n\\\\n**Supporting evidence:**\\\\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: 99.83% (Tier 1 exception)\\\\n- `vota_ae_only_seed42`: 99.92% (Tier 1 assertion-point, 与 exception 统计等价 p=0.80)\\\\n- `vota_loop_only_seed42`: 85.6% (Tier 2 behavioral)\\\\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (≈ loop_only, confirming extraction equivalence)\\\\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: 99.33% (Tier 1 cross-model validation)\\\\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (Tier 1+2 cross-model: Tier 2 introduces model-dependent degradation)\\\\n- Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, VOTA ECE=0.0054 (Tier 1 better calibrated)\\\\n\\\\n**Design principle**: verdict-optimized traces should extract highest-specificity diagnostic signals; lower-specificity signals are redundant and may introduce noise.\\\\n\\\\n## Story Spine\\\\n\\\\n- **Problem**: Existing trace SFT studies (DCSH, TracePile) evaluate only generative tasks and find limited, model-dependent benefit. No prior work has tested trace SFT on verdict tasks (binary pass/fail), nor identified what trace components drive classification performance.\\\\n\\\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: DCSH (EMNLP Findings 2025) tested 5 trace formats on generation tasks, finding that \\\\\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others.\\\\\\\" TracePile (NeurIPS 2025) scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation. TRACED (ICSE 2024) showed traces help encoder-based classification. All existing trace formats are designed for execution simulation, not fault detection.\\\\n\\\\n- **Our framing**: We reframe verdict prediction as **fault detection** and ask: what is the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal? Through systematic component ablation across two architecturally distinct models, we discover that exception events — compact, structured error summaries — are both necessary and sufficient, achieving near-perfect accuracy (99.5%+ avg) with near-zero cross-model gap. Richer trace representations work only when the base model has sufficient execution reasoning capability, making them unreliable for general deployment.\\\\n\\\\n- **Headline result**: \\\\\\\"Exception-based trace SFT achieves 99.83% verdict accuracy on Qwen and 99.33% on DeepSeek (gap = 0.50pp), while the same models show a 14.96pp gap on VOTA traces — demonstrating that fault detection, not trace reasoning, is the robust mechanism for verdict prediction.\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n## Story Arc\\\\n\\\\n1. **Setup**: Trace SFT for code tasks — promising but inconsistent in prior work (DCSH, TracePile). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\\\\n\\\\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.1% → full_trace 81.8% → VOTA 99.41%. CRUXEval reasoning gap (17.5pp) parallels VOTA gap — trace utilization is bounded by pretraining.\\\\n\\\\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\\\\n\\\\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\\\\n\\\\n**现状**: DeepSeek full_trace 28.5%, 100% 预测 fail. 根因 (D018): evaluate.py predict() 缺少截断逻辑, 推理时输入远超训练分布导致 mode collapse.\\\\n\\\\n**处理方案**: 修复重跑 in progress (D019). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\\n\\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.1% → full_trace 81.8% → VOTA 99.41%. Label-only loss ablation decomposes the gap: 26% gradient dilution, 74% information overload. Compression improves accuracy through higher information density, not just cost savings.\\n\\n3. **Twist — what component actually drives VOTA?** Component ablation reveals exception_only (99.83%) ≥ full VOTA (99.41%). The \\\"trace reasoning\\\" narrative is wrong — verdict prediction is fault detection. Tier 1 (exceptions + assertion-point) subsumes Tier 2 (loops). Actual/expected removal doesn't hurt (99.41%).\\n\\n4. **Cross-model stress test — the universal vs the contingent**: Exception_only transfers with near-zero gap (0.50pp), but VOTA degrades by 14.96pp on DeepSeek. The model-agnostic component is precisely the one that doesn't require trace reasoning. CRUXEval reasoning gap (17.5pp) parallels VOTA gap — trace utilization is bounded by pretraining.\\n\\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\\n\\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\\n\\n**现状**: DeepSeek full_trace 28.5%, 100% 预测 fail. 根因 (D018): evaluate.py predict() 缺少截断逻辑, 推理时输入远超训练分布导致 mode collapse.\\n\\n**处理方案**: 修复重跑 in progress (D019). Fix Worker 正在修正 evaluate.py 截断逻辑 + 重评 3 个 checkpoint. 如果修复结果在 submission 前就绪, 纳入跨模型对比表 (4/4 conditions). 如果时间不够, 报告为 N/A 并在 Limitations 中透明披露根因.\\n\\n**对论文叙事的影响**: 无. 三个有效条件 (exception_only, VOTA, no_trace) 已足够支撑全部 claims. DeepSeek full_trace 结果是 bonus, 非必需.\\n\\n## Venue Fit Risks\\n\\n| Risk | Assessment | Mitigation |\\n|------|-----------|------------|\\n| 单任务 | 仍然只在 test verdict prediction 上验证 | 跨模型验证部分弥补; Discussion 中讨论 generalization |\\n| 单数据集 | CRUXEval 是唯一 benchmark | 定位为 empirical study, 深度优于广度 |\\n| \\\"只是 feature engineering\\\" | exception extraction 不复杂 | simplicity 本身是 contribution — 最简信号最 robust |\\n| Negative result 味道 | VOTA 跨模型不 robust | Reframe: 识别 robust vs non-robust 组件边界是 positive |\\n| Seed robustness | 核心 claims 当前单 seed | plan_008 补充中; 两模型一致性是 implicit robustness |\\n\\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\\n\\n| Concern | Planned Response |\\n|---------|-----------------|\\n| \\\"exception_only 99.83% 是不是 trivial pattern matching [ERROR] 有无？\\\" | full_trace 也包含 [ERROR] 但仅 81.8%——模型需要从 trace context 中 extract 和 utilize exception signal, 不是简单 keyword detection. no_trace 无 exception, 75.1%. |\\n| \\\"为什么 exception_only > VOTA? adding info 反而降?\\\" | 非 exception 组件 (loop/ae tokens) 稀释 attention 对 exception 信号的聚焦. Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027 vs VOTA ECE=0.0054. 部分样本 loop 正常但有 exception, 引入矛盾信号. |\\n| \\\"CRUXEval gap 与 VOTA gap 的相关性可能是 coincidence\\\" | 明确标注为 hypothesis. 列出 alternatives (tokenizer efficiency, pretraining scale 2.75x, chat template). 不 overclaim. |\\n| \\\"只有 2 个模型\\\" | 同意是 limitation. 两模型在 architecture, pretraining data (5.5T vs 2T), execution reasoning (CRUXEval 17.5pp gap) 上有充分差异, 不是 trivial replication. |\\n| \\\"99.83% 可能 saturated, 看不出 exception 到底学了什么\\\" | Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, ae_only ECE=0.0004. 模型对 pass/fail 的置信度分布有意义差异. |\\n| \\\"只用了 1 个 seed\\\" | plan_008 补充中. 两模型独立达到 99%+ 本身是 implicit 2-seed validation. |\\n| \\\"eval_loss 上升但 accuracy 提升——checkpoint selection 有问题?\\\" | Discussion: token-level loss 与 binary verdict accuracy 解耦. 推荐 verdict-specific metric. Methodological contribution. |\\n| \\\"DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌是 bug 不是 finding\\\" | 诚实披露根因 (evaluate.py 截断缺失). 其他 3 条件不受影响. 修复版本如就绪则补入. |\\n\\n## Discussion 要点\\n\\n### D1: eval_loss vs verdict accuracy 解耦\\nDeepSeek VOTA: eval_loss epoch1→3 从 0.068 升至 0.112 (+65%), 但 accuracy 从 78.15% 升至 84.45% (+6.3pp). Token-level calibration 与 binary classification accuracy 方向相反. **Implication**: verdict tasks 应用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1) 做 checkpoint selection, 非 eval_loss.\\n\\n### D2: CRUXEval gap 假说\\nVOTA 跨模型 gap (14.96pp) 与 CRUXEval execution reasoning gap (15-17.5pp) 数值吻合. 假说: trace utilization effectiveness 受 base model \\\"execution reasoning readiness\\\" 约束. **标注**: hypothesis, N=2, 列出 alternatives. 不 overclaim.\\n\\n### D3: Simplicity as robustness\\nException signal 跨模型 robust 恰因其 structurally simple. 暗示 **trace design 应匹配 deployment model 的能力下界**, 而非上界.\\n\\n### D4: Checkpoint selection 与 gap 报告\\n正文 exception_only 跨模型 gap 用 final-to-final = 0.50pp (一致 criterion). 补充: at best checkpoint (DeepSeek epoch1), gap = 0pp. DeepSeek epoch1→3 退化 0.50pp 与 D1 eval_loss 解耦现象一致.\\n\\n## Contingency Framing (已解决, 留存档)\\n\\n### Claim 1 — RESOLVED (D008 → D010 → D019)\\nvota_no_ae 99.41% 否定 trivial comparison. exception_only 99.83% 揭示核心机制. 跨模型 0.50pp gap 确认 universality.\\n\\n### Claim 2 — RESOLVED (D006 + D009)\\nv5 full_trace 81.8%. plan_002 label-only 86.2%, gradient dilution = minor (26%). 跨模型 VOTA gap 补充证据.\\n\\n## 与 v1 Framing 的关键差异\\n\\n| 维度 | v1 | v2.1 |\\n|------|----|----|\\n| Headline | VOTA compression 优于 full trace | Exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic |\\n| Primary claim | VOTA +24pp through fault indicator preservation | Exception signal transfers across models (gap = 0.50pp) |\\n| Claim 2 | Compression > full trace (只有 gradient dilution) | Information density primary + gradient dilution quantification + cross-model reinforcement |\\n| Claim 3 Tier 1 | exception_only only | exception + assertion-point (co-Tier 1, D020) |\\n| Model scope | 单模型 (Qwen) | 双模型, 跨模型对比是核心贡献 |\\n| VOTA 定位 | 核心方法 | Model-dependent richer representation |\\n| DeepSeek full_trace | N/A | Fix in progress (D019); N/A fallback |\\n\", \"total_chars\": 11959, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

pattern matching 而非理解 trace\\\\n2. **Label 截断**：full_trace 28.4% 是因为 label 在序列末尾被 tokenizer 截断，模型根本没学到正确标签\\\\n\\\\n### 教训\\\\n- VOTA 输出中任何直接或间接暴露 pass/fail 的字段都是 data leakage——审查 trace format 时需要\\\\\\\"如果模型只看这一个字段能否猜到答案\\\\\\\"的测试\\\\n- 训练数据格式化必须保证 label 不被截断——应先预留 label token budget，再分配 trace 空间\\\\n- `[No trace available - execution failed]` 这类文本也是隐性泄漏（暗示 fail）\\\\n\\\\n## MVP v5 + Ablation 结果（当前有效数据）[2026-05-15]\\\\n\\\\n### 三条件对比（全部 v5 代码版本，统一 bootstrap CI）\\\\n- no_trace v5: 75.1% [72.8, 77.5], lift +3.6pp over majority\\\\n- **full_trace v5: 81.8%** [79.7, 83.9], lift +6.6pp [4.4, 9.0] vs no_trace\\\\n- **VOTA v5: 99.41%** [98.8, 99.8], lift +24.3pp [21.8, 26.8] vs no_trace (accuracy corrected from 99.16% due to Run 4 adapter overwrite)\\\\n- **VOTA vs full_trace: +17.7pp** [15.5, 19.8]（所有对比 p < 0.001）\\\\n\\\\n### plan_001 ablation: VOTA w/o actual/expected [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **accuracy 99.4%** [98.9, 99.8] — 去掉 actual/expected 后性能不降反微升（vs VOTA v5 99.2%）\\\\n- **结论：actual/expected 字段是冗余信息**，VOTA 效果来自 trace 行为模式理解\\\\n- Claim 1 走 **full positive framing**: \\\\\\\"VOTA teaches execution-grounded verdict reasoning\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### plan_002 ablation: label-only loss on full_trace [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **accuracy 86.2%** [84.4, 88.1] — 比标准 full_trace v5 (81.8%) 提升 +4.5pp\\\\n- **结论：full_trace-VOTA gap 的主因是 information overload（trace 噪声），不是 gradient dilution**\\\\n  - gradient dilution 贡献: ~4.5pp | information overload 贡献: ~13pp\\\\n\\\\n### plan_003a ablation: exception_only [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **accuracy 99.83%** — pass_F1 0.999, fail_F1 0.997\\\\n- **[ERROR] 事件是 VOTA verdict 判断的主驱动因素**\\\\n\\\\n### plan_003b ablation: loop_only [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **accuracy 85.6%** [83.5, 87.6]\\\\n- **结论：loop compression 提供中等贡献，但 exception 是决定性组件**\\\\n\\\\n### plan_003c ablation: ae_only [2026-05-15] — Tier 1 diagnostic-moment signal\\\\n- **accuracy 99.92%** [99.75%, 100%] — 与 exception_only (99.83%) 统计等价\\\\n- D013 裁定：不是 data leakage\\\\n- Pairwise bootstrap: vs exception_only p=0.80 ❌ 统计等价\\\\n\\\\n### 完整组件重要性层级（D013 确认）[2026-05-15]\\\\n```\\\\nae_only         99.92%  Tier 1\\\\nexception_only  99.83%  Tier 1\\\\nVOTA w/o ae     99.4%\\\\nVOTA (full)     99.41%\\\\nlabel-only      86.2%\\\\nloop_only       85.6%\\\\nfull_trace      81.8%\\\\nno_trace        75.1%\\\\nmajority        71.5%\\\\n```\\\\n\\\\n### plan_007 calibration analysis v2（P0/P1/P2 修复后）[2026-05-15]\\\\n| Condition | ECE ↓ | MCE | Brier ↓ |\\\\n|-----------|-------|-----|---------|\\\\n| ae_only | 0.0004 | 0.0004 | 0.0008 |\\\\n| exception_only | 0.0027 | 0.8355 | 0.0013 |\\\\n| vota | 0.0054 | 0.6225 | 0.0052 |\\\\n| loop_only | 0.0831 | 0.1834 | 0.1183 |\\\\n| full_trace | 0.0958 | 0.2491 | 0.1439 |\\\\n| label_only | 0.1107 | 0.4171 | 0.1201 |\\\\n| no_trace | 0.1715 | 0.4756 | 0.1972 |\\\\n- P0: token ID 动态查询（DeepSeek calibration 必须用 v2 代码）\\\\n- P1: greedy generation 对齐（~3 样本差异）\\\\n- P2: loop_only MCE null → 0.1834\\\\n\\\\n### plan_004 跨模型验证: DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B [2026-05-15]\\\\n\\\\n#### 跨模型对比表（4/4 完成）✅\\\\n| Condition | Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B | DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | Δ |\\\\n|-----------|-------------------|---------------------|---|\\\\n| no_trace | 75.13% | 73.36% | -1.77pp (不显著) |\\\\n| full_trace | 81.76% | 28.5% (collapsed) | -53.3pp |\\\\n| VOTA | 99.41% | 84.45% | -14.96pp |\\\\n| **exception_only** | **99.83%** | **99.33%** | **-0.50pp** |\\\\n\\\\n#### DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- 28.5% accuracy, 100% 预测 fail (pass_recall=0, fail_recall=1)\\\\n- 同 Qwen v2 full_trace 坍塌模式 — chat template overhead 导致 label 截断\\\\n- 诊断中：所有条件共享用 Qwen tokenizer overhead=32 格式化的数据，DeepSeek chat template overhead 不同\\\\n- **不影响其他 3 条件**：no_trace/VOTA/exception_only 输入远短于 full_trace，不触发截断\\\\n- DeepSeek epoch-level: eval_loss epoch2→3 从 0.090 升至 0.110，train_loss 极低 (0.052) — 模型 overfit 但 generation 坍塌\\\\n\\\\n#### ⭐ 关键发现：exception signal 跨模型 near-universal [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **exception_only 跨模型 gap 仅 0.50pp**（99.83% vs 99.33%），而 VOTA gap 14.96pp\\\\n- VOTA 退化到 84.45% 说明 loop/ae compression 对不同 tokenizer/architecture 不 robust\\\\n- 但 exception signal extraction 本身是 **model-agnostic** 的\\\\n- **论文 headline 应围绕 exception signal，不是全 VOTA 压缩**\\\\n- Director 决策：exception_only seed robustness 优先于 VOTA seed robustness\\\\n- DeepSeek exception_only epoch1 = 99.83%（与 Qwen 完全一致），epoch3 = 99.33%（轻微 overfitting）\\\\n\\\\n#### DeepSeek VOTA 详细数据\\\\n- **DeepSeek VOTA accuracy: 84.45%** [82.6%, 86.4%]，使用 **epoch 3 final** checkpoint\\\\n- **DeepSeek no_trace accuracy: 73.36%** [70.8%, 75.7%]，lift +1.85pp over majority (**不显著**, CI [-0.9, +4.6])\\\\n- **VOTA lift**: DeepSeek +11.09pp [+7.90, +14.37] vs Qwen +24.03pp — 跨模型泛化确认但幅度衰减（pairwise bootstrap p < 0.0001）\\\\n- **Pass 偏向严重**: VOTA fail recall 仅 45.4%\\\\n- **DeepSeek exception_only: 99.33%** — within-model lift +25.97pp over no_trace\\\\n\\\\n#### ⚠️ Bootstrap 方法一致性待修（非紧急）[2026-05-15]\\\\n- Qwen 实验用 **paired** bootstrap（有 per-sample predictions）\\\\n- DeepSeek 当前用 **unpaired** bootstrap（缺 per-sample predictions）→ CI 更宽，是保守估计\\\\n- **论文同一张表不能混用两种方法**\\\\n- **待办**: 为 deepseek_vota_seed42 和 deepseek_no_trace_seed42 重跑 `evaluate.py --save_predictions`，再用 paired bootstrap 重算 CI\\\\n- 当前 unpaired 结果 (+11.09pp [+7.90, +14.37]) 方向正确，paired CI 只会更窄\\\\n\\\\n#### eval_loss 与 verdict accuracy 解耦（关键发现）[2026-05-15]\\\\n- Epoch 1: eval_loss 0.068, accuracy **78.15%**, fail_recall **23.3%**\\\\n- Epoch 3: eval_loss 0.112, accuracy **84.45%**, fail_recall **45.4%**\\\\n- eval_loss 上升 (+65%) 但 accuracy 提升 (+6.3pp), fail_recall 大幅提升 (+22.1pp)\\\\n- **结论**：eval_loss（token-level calibration）与 binary classification accuracy 解耦。eval_loss 不是 verdict task 的可靠 checkpoint selection criterion\\\\n- **论文价值**: Discussion 中应讨论此现象\\\\n\\\\n### `load_best_model_at_end` 代码版本时间线 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- **13:24 之前**的所有实验：使用 epoch 3 final checkpoint（包括全部 Qwen + deepseek_vota/no_trace/exception_only）\\\\n- **13:24 Worker 新增** L149-152：load_best_model_at_end=True 等 4 行\\\\n- **deepseek_full_trace 必须用旧代码**（已注释为 # TEMP_DISABLED），保持 DeepSeek 组内一致\\\\n- **Qwen seed 实验**可用新代码（Qwen 组内一致即可）\\\\n\\\\n### 关键结论修正 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- v2 full_trace 28.4% 根因是 chat template overhead bug，v5 修复后 81.8%\\\\n\\\\n### 决策教训 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- 判断 data leakage 必须看原始数据内容，不能仅凭比率+accuracy 异常推断\\\\n\\\\n## DeepSeek vs Qwen trace utilization gap — 文献解释（HYPOTHESIS，非 definitive）[2026-05-15]\\\\n\\\\n### CRUXEval 执行推理能力差距\\\\n| Model | CRUXEval-I (CoT) | CRUXEval-O (CoT) |\\\\n|-------|-------------------|-------------------|\\\\n| Qwen2.5-Coder-7B | 56.5% | 56.0% |\\\\n| DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | 39.0% | 41.0% |\\\\n| **差距** | **+17.5pp** | **+15.0pp** |\\\\n\\\\nCRUXEval 15-17pp 差距与 VOTA accuracy 14.75pp 差距吻合。\\\\n\\\\n### 关键文献\\\\n1. Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\\\\\\\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\\\\\\\"\\\\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\\\\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \\\\\\\"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\\\\\\\"\\\\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \\\\\\\"trace readiness\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\\\\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑\\\\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\\\\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\\\\n\\\\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\\\\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\\\\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\\\\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\\\\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \\\\\\\"1.17T\\\\\\\" 和 \\\\\\\"4.7x\\\\\\\" 为错误数据，不得引用\\\\n\\\\n#### ⭐ 新视角：exception signal 免疫于 trace utilization gap [2026-05-15]\\\\n- VOTA 跨模型 gap 14.96pp → exception_only gap 仅 0.50pp\\\\n- 解释：exception events 结构简单（[ERROR] type: ... message: ...），不需要深度 trace reasoning 能力\\\\n- CRUXEval 差距只影响需要理解复杂 trace 结构（loop patterns, variable flow）的组件\\\\n- **论文可 claim**: exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic; trace complexity tolerance varies by model capability\\\\n\\\\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\\\\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\\\\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\\\\n- \\\\\\\"pass\\\\\\\"/\\\\\\\"fail\\\\\\\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\\\\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\\\\n\\\\n## 流程教训\\\\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\\\\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\\\\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\\\\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\\\\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\\\\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\\\\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\\\\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\\\\\\\"eval 几分钟就完\\\\\\\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\\\\\\\"原有\\\\\\\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\\\\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\\\\n\\\\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\\\\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\\\\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\\\\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\\\\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\\\\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\\\\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\\\\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 9134, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

28.4% 根因是 chat template overhead bug，v5 修复后 81.8%\n\n### 决策教训 [2026-05-15]\n- 判断 data leakage 必须看原始数据内容，不能仅凭比率+accuracy 异常推断\n\n## DeepSeek vs Qwen trace utilization gap — 文献解释（HYPOTHESIS，非 definitive）[2026-05-15]\n\n### CRUXEval 执行推理能力差距\n| Model | CRUXEval-I (CoT) | CRUXEval-O (CoT) |\n|-------|-------------------|-------------------|\n| Qwen2.5-Coder-7B | 56.5% | 56.0% |\n| DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B | 39.0% | 41.0% |\n| **差距** | **+17.5pp** | **+15.0pp** |\n\nCRUXEval 15-17pp 差距与 VOTA accuracy 14.75pp 差距吻合。\n\n### 关键文献\n1. Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): trace SFT 效果因模型而异，\"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others\"\n2. Ferrando et al. (2025): compact scratchpad ≥ full scratchpad\n3. Gao et al. (2025): \"base model inductive bias outweighs SFT corpus\"\n4. Meta CWM (2025): execution trace mid-training 需要 base model \"trace readiness\"\n\n### Discussion 假说（论文 Discussion 中应呈现为 hypothesis，列出 alternative explanations）\n- H1: Execution reasoning readiness（CRUXEval gap 预测 VOTA gap）— 最有文献支撑\n- H2: Pretraining scale × structured data（Qwen 5.5T vs DeepSeek 2T tokens, 2.75x）— 合理但不能作为 definitive conclusion\n- H3: Base model inductive bias ceiling（train loss 相当但泛化差距大）\n\n#### Alternative explanations（论文 Discussion 必须列出）\n- Tokenizer 效率差异（不同 tokenizer 对 trace 文本的编码效率可能不同）\n- Chat template 差异（不同模型的 chat template 对 trace 格式的适配程度不同）\n- Instruction tuning 数据质量差异（RLHF/DPO 阶段的对齐方式可能影响 SFT 下游任务）\n- ⚠️ 数据修正：DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B 预训练 **2T tokens**（87% code + 13% NL），来源 DeepSeek-Coder 技术报告。cross-model-trace-lit Worker 产出的 \"1.17T\" 和 \"4.7x\" 为错误数据，不得引用\n\n#### ⭐ 新视角：exception signal 免疫于 trace utilization gap [2026-05-15]\n- VOTA 跨模型 gap 14.96pp → exception_only gap 仅 0.50pp\n- 解释：exception events 结构简单（[ERROR] type: ... message: ...），不需要深度 trace reasoning 能力\n- CRUXEval 差距只影响需要理解复杂 trace 结构（loop patterns, variable flow）的组件\n- **论文可 claim**: exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic; trace complexity tolerance varies by model capability\n\n## 环境信息 [2026-05-14]\n- westd-11235: 2x RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 94GB, CUDA 12.8\n- 模型通过 ModelScope CDN 下载\n- \"pass\"/\"fail\" 在 Qwen/DeepSeek tokenizer 中均为单 token\n- Qwen2.5-Coder chat template overhead: 31 tokens\n\n## 流程教训\n- submit_training_job 必须用，不能用 nohup\n- Worker 汇报需要交叉验证（exit code + log）\n- [2026-05-15] 数据格式化 token budget 必须扣除 chat template overhead\n- [2026-05-15] 所有训练必须加 --gradient_checkpointing\n- [2026-05-15] HuggingFace 下载设 HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1，优先 ModelScope CDN\n- [2026-05-15] Worker 长时间无产出必须主动 get_worker_status(verbose=True) 排查\n- [2026-05-15] bash -c 内部必须显式 export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES={N}\n- [2026-05-15] GPU 碰撞：提交前 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 无其他进程\n- [2026-05-15] **同 GPU 并行 eval+training 严重降速**：cuda:1 上 eval+training 并行导致训练从 7s/step 恶化到 35-49s/step。eval 必须用独立 GPU 或等训练完成。不要因\"eval 几分钟就完\"而在同一 GPU 上并行\n- [2026-05-15] **重复 Worker 竞态**：两个同名 Worker 相隔 30 秒启动，后者看到前者的修改误判为\"原有\"。派 Worker 前查 list_workers 确认无重复任务\n- [2026-05-15] **Bootstrap 方法一致性**：同一论文表格中所有 pairwise CI 必须用同一方法（paired 或 unpaired）。evaluate.py 必须加 --save_predictions 保存 per-sample 结果\n\n## 文献定位 [2026-05-15]\n- Wang et al. (EMNLP 2025): 原始 trace 对 SFT 帮助有限\n- APPT (TSE 2024): 最近 competitor，BERT 非 decoder，无 trace\n- **文献空白**：无现有工作做 compressed execution trace + SFT for binary verdict\n- Ferrando et al. 2025: compact ≥ full scratchpad\n- TraceCoder (ICSE 2026): 选择性 probe 优于全量 trace\n- TraceRepair (2026): critical variable snapshots > 全量\n- Demystifying Errors (2025): LLM 值追踪能力弱\n"

{
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "file": "paper_framing.md",
  "content": "# Paper Framing — trace-verdict-study\n\n> v2.2 — 更新 seed robustness 结果 + irreducible samples + DeepSeek full_trace v2 状态。\n\n## Target Venue\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)\n- Fallback: EMNLP 2026 Findings\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026\n\n## Core Insight\n\nVerdict prediction (pass/fail) is fundamentally a **fault detection** task. The minimal sufficient signal is the **exception event** — a compact, structured diagnostic moment that achieves near-perfect accuracy (Qwen 99.83%, DeepSeek 99.33%, cross-model gap = 0.50pp) regardless of model architecture. In contrast, richer trace representations that require deeper execution reasoning show model-dependent effectiveness: VOTA gap = 14.96pp, no_trace gap = 1.77pp. Information density — not training signal distribution — is the primary bottleneck: even oracle loss masking (label-only) cannot close the 13pp gap between full traces and targeted extraction. The design principle: **extract the highest-specificity diagnostic signal; discard everything else.**\n\n## Core Claims (3)\n\n### Claim 1: Exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic\n\nException summaries alone achieve near-perfect verdict prediction across architecturally distinct models: Qwen-2.5-Coder-7B (99.83%) and DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B (99.33%), cross-model gap = 0.50pp (at best checkpoint, gap = 0pp). This contrasts with VOTA (gap = 14.96pp) and no_trace (gap = 1.77pp). Exception events are structurally simple (`[ERROR] type: ... message: ...`) and do not require deep trace reasoning — making them universally extractable.\n\n**Supporting evidence:**\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: Qwen 99.83%\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: DeepSeek 99.33% (epoch3) / 99.83% (epoch1 best)\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: DeepSeek VOTA 84.45% (gap = 14.96pp)\n- `deepseek_no_trace_seed42`: DeepSeek no_trace 73.36% (gap = 1.77pp)\n- `vota_no_ae_seed42`: 99.41% — actual/expected removal 不降，非 trivial string comparison\n\n**Seed robustness** ✅: Qwen exception_only 99.83% ± 0.00 across seeds 42/123/456. 每 seed 恰好 2/1190 errors, 3 seeds 错误 100% 重叠（id=901, 905, 均为 `check_dict_case` 函数, label=pass → pred=fail）。**Irreducible samples**: 模型已达数据集理论上限。\n\n**DeepSeek cross-model seed robustness**: deepseek_exception_only_seed123 运行中（~20:15 UTC 完成）。\n\n### Claim 2: Information density, not gradient dilution, drives trace compression benefit\n\nFull traces help verdict prediction (81.8%, +6.6pp over baseline), but VOTA achieves 99.41% (+24.3pp) — a +17.6pp advantage. Label-only loss ablation (plan_002) quantifies the causal decomposition: **gradient dilution contributes ~26% of the gap** (4.5pp; label-only full_trace = 86.2%), **information overload contributes ~74%** (13.0pp; only trace compression resolves this). Even with oracle loss masking that eliminates gradient dilution, full traces still underperform VOTA by 13pp.\n\nCross-model evidence reinforces this: VOTA gap (14.96pp) tracks base model execution reasoning capability (Qwen CRUXEval-I 56.5% vs DeepSeek 39.0%, Δ=17.5pp), suggesting trace utilization effectiveness is bounded by pretraining-derived inductive biases. Exception signal bypasses this bottleneck entirely (gap = 0.50pp).\n\n**Supporting evidence:**\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (gradient dilution fix, +4.5pp over standard full_trace)\n- `full_trace_seed42_v5`: 81.8% (standard full_trace)\n- `vota_seed42_v5`: 99.41%\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (VOTA model-dependent)\n- CRUXEval literature data (Wang et al.)\n\n### Claim 3: Two-tier diagnostic hierarchy for verdict prediction\n\nComponent ablation reveals verdict-relevant trace information has a strict two-tier hierarchy:\n- **Tier 1 — diagnostic moment signals**: exception summaries (99.83%) and assertion-point information (99.92%) are individually near-perfect and sufficient for verdict prediction. These signals are structurally simple and model-agnostic (exception cross-model gap = 0.50pp).\n- **Tier 2 — behavioral patterns**: loop compression (85.6%) carries substantial signal but is model-dependent and fully subsumed by Tier 1.\n\nCombining tiers introduces noise rather than helping: exception_only (99.83%) > VOTA full (99.41%), p=0.01. Loop_only ≈ full_trace_label_only (85.6% vs 86.2%) confirms VOTA's loop compression precisely extracts the useful behavioral signal from full traces.\n\n**Supporting evidence:**\n- `vota_exception_only_seed42_v2`: 99.83% (Tier 1 exception)\n- `vota_ae_only_seed42`: 99.92% (Tier 1 assertion-point, 与 exception 统计等价 p=0.80)\n- `vota_loop_only_seed42`: 85.6% (Tier 2 behavioral)\n- `full_trace_label_only_seed42`: 86.2% (≈ loop_only, confirming extraction equivalence)\n- `deepseek_exception_only_seed42`: 99.33% (Tier 1 cross-model validation)\n- `deepseek_vota_seed42`: 84.45% (Tier 1+2 cross-model: Tier 2 introduces model-dependent degradation)\n- Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, VOTA ECE=0.0054 (Tier 1 better calibrated)\n\n**Design principle**: verdict-optimized traces should extract highest-specificity diagnostic signals; lower-specificity signals are redundant and may introduce noise.\n\n## Story Spine\n\n- **Problem**: Existing trace SFT studies (DCSH, TracePile) evaluate only generative tasks and find limited, model-dependent benefit. No prior work has tested trace SFT on verdict tasks (binary pass/fail), nor identified what trace components drive classification performance.\n\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: DCSH (EMNLP Findings 2025) tested 5 trace formats on generation tasks, finding that \"no single trace representation consistently outperforms others.\" TracePile (NeurIPS 2025) scaled to 2.6M samples but only evaluated generation. TRACED (ICSE 2024) showed traces help encoder-based classification. All existing trace formats are designed for execution simulation, not fault detection.\n\n- **Our framing**: We reframe verdict prediction as **fault detection** and ask: what is the minimal sufficient diagnostic signal? Through systematic component ablation across two architecturally distinct models, we discover that exception events — compact, structured error summaries — are both necessary and sufficient, achieving near-perfect accuracy (99.5%+ avg) with near-zero cross-model gap. Richer trace representations work only when the base model has sufficient execution reasoning capability, making them unreliable for general deployment.\n\n- **Headline result**: \"Exception-based trace SFT achieves 99.83% verdict accuracy on Qwen and 99.33% on DeepSeek (gap = 0.50pp), while the same models show a 14.96pp gap on VOTA traces — demonstrating that fault detection, not trace reasoning, is the robust mechanism for verdict prediction.\"\n\n## Story Arc\n\n1. **Setup**: Trace SFT for code tasks — promising but inconsistent in prior work (DCSH, TracePile). Unknown whether traces help verdict tasks and what trace design matters.\n\n2. **First result — traces help, compression helps more** (single model): no_trace 75.1% → full_trace 81.8% → VOTA 99.41%. Label-only loss ablation decomposes the gap: 26% gradient dilution, 74% information overload. Compression improves accuracy through higher information density, not just cost savings.\n\n3. **Twist — what component actually drives VOTA?** Component ablation reveals exception_only (99.83%) ≥ full VOTA (99.41%). The \"trace reasoning\" narrative is wrong — verdict prediction is fault detection. Tier 1 (exceptions + assertion-point) subsumes Tier 2 (loops). Actual/expected removal doesn't hurt (99.41%).\n\n4. **Cross-model stress test — the universal vs the contingent**: Exception_only transfers with near-zero gap (0.50pp), but VOTA degrades by 14.96pp on DeepSeek. The model-agnostic component is precisely the one that doesn't require trace reasoning. CRUXEval reasoning gap (17.5pp) parallels VOTA gap — trace utilization is bounded by pretraining.\n\n5. **Design principle**: For verdict tasks, identify the highest-specificity diagnostic signal (exception events) and discard everything else. Simpler signals are more robust; complexity tolerance depends on model capability.\n\n## DeepSeek full_trace 处理\n\n**现状**: v1 坍塌（28.5%, 100% 预测 fail, 根因: 训练端 label 截断）。v2 重训启动但被 kill（Loop #6 ops worker 腾 GPU 给 vota_seed456）。\n\n**处理方案**: 下一个 GPU 空闲（deepseek_exception_only_seed123 完成后 ~20:15 UTC）立即重启 deepseek_full_trace_v3。\n\n**对论文叙事的影响**: 无. 三个有效条件 (exception_only, VOTA, no_trace) 已足够支撑全部 claims. DeepSeek full_trace 结果是 bonus, 非必需. 若 v3 在投稿前完成, 纳入跨模型对比表 (4/4 conditions). 否则报告为 N/A 并在 Limitations 中透明披露根因.\n\n## Venue Fit Risks\n\n| Risk | Assessment | Mitigation |\n|------|-----------|------------|\n| 单任务 | 仍然只在 test verdict prediction 上验证 | 跨模型验证部分弥补; Discussion 中讨论 generalization |\n| 单数据集 | CRUXEval 是唯一 benchmark | 定位为 empirical study, 深度优于广度 |\n| \"只是 feature engineering\" | exception extraction 不复杂 | simplicity 本身是 contribution — 最简信号最 robust |\n| Negative result 味道 | VOTA 跨模型不 robust | Reframe: 识别 robust vs non-robust 组件边界是 positive |\n| Seed robustness | ✅ 已解决: 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds), irreducible samples 确认 | — |\n\n## Anticipated Reviewer Concerns\n\n| Concern | Planned Response |\n|---------|-----------------|\n| \"exception_only 99.83% 是不是 trivial pattern matching [ERROR] 有无？\" | full_trace 也包含 [ERROR] 但仅 81.8%——模型需要从 trace context 中 extract 和 utilize exception signal, 不是简单 keyword detection. no_trace 无 exception, 75.1%. |\n| \"为什么 exception_only > VOTA? adding info 反而降?\" | 非 exception 组件 (loop/ae tokens) 稀释 attention 对 exception 信号的聚焦. Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027 vs VOTA ECE=0.0054. 部分样本 loop 正常但有 exception, 引入矛盾信号. |\n| \"CRUXEval gap 与 VOTA gap 的相关性可能是 coincidence\" | 明确标注为 hypothesis. 列出 alternatives (tokenizer efficiency, pretraining scale 2.75x, chat template). 不 overclaim. |\n| \"只有 2 个模型\" | 同意是 limitation. 两模型在 architecture, pretraining data (5.5T vs 2T), execution reasoning (CRUXEval 17.5pp gap) 上有充分差异, 不是 trivial replication. |\n| \"99.83% 可能 saturated, 看不出 exception 到底学了什么\" | Calibration: exception_only ECE=0.0027, ae_only ECE=0.0004. Irreducible samples (id=901,905) 分析说明模型已达数据上限. |\n| \"只用了 1 个 seed\" | ✅ 已解决: 3 seeds 结果完全一致, irreducible samples 100% 重叠. |\n| \"eval_loss 上升但 accuracy 提升——checkpoint selection 有问题?\" | Discussion: token-level loss 与 binary verdict accuracy 解耦. 推荐 verdict-specific metric. Methodological contribution. |\n| \"DeepSeek full_trace 坍塌是 bug 不是 finding\" | 诚实披露根因 (训练端 label 截断). 其他 3 条件不受影响. 修复版本如就绪则补入. |\n\n## Discussion 要点\n\n### D1: eval_loss vs verdict accuracy 解耦\nDeepSeek VOTA: eval_loss epoch1→3 从 0.068 升至 0.112 (+65%), 但 accuracy 从 78.15% 升至 84.45% (+6.3pp). Token-level calibration 与 binary classification accuracy 方向相反. **Implication**: verdict tasks 应用 task-specific metric (accuracy/F1) 做 checkpoint selection, 非 eval_loss.\n\n### D2: CRUXEval gap 假说\nVOTA 跨模型 gap (14.96pp) 与 CRUXEval execution reasoning gap (15-17.5pp) 数值吻合. 假说: trace utilization effectiveness 受 base model \"execution reasoning readiness\" 约束. **标注**: hypothesis, N=2, 列出 alternatives. 不 overclaim.\n\n### D3: Simplicity as robustness\nException signal 跨模型 robust 恰因其 structurally simple. 暗示 **trace design 应匹配 deployment model 的能力下界**, 而非上界.\n\n### D4: Checkpoint selection 与 gap 报告\n正文 exception_only 跨模型 gap 用 final-to-final = 0.50pp (一致 criterion). 补充: at best checkpoint (DeepSeek epoch1), gap = 0pp. DeepSeek epoch1→3 退化 0.50pp 与 D1 eval_loss 解耦现象一致.\n\n### D5: Irreducible samples\n3 seeds 的 2/1190 errors 完全相同（id=901, 905, `check_dict_case`），方向一致（pass→fail false negatives）。模型已达数据集理论上限。这些样本可能存在 labeling noise 或 edge-case 行为（exception trace 中缺少足够的 fault signal）。\n\n## Contingency Framing (已解决, 留存档)\n\n### Claim 1 — RESOLVED (D008 → D010 → D019)\nvota_no_ae 99.41% 否定 trivial comparison. exception_only 99.83% 揭示核心机制. 跨模型 0.50pp gap 确认 universality.\n\n### Claim 2 — RESOLVED (D006 + D009)\nv5 full_trace 81.8%. plan_002 label-only 86.2%, gradient dilution = minor (26%). 跨模型 VOTA gap 补充证据.\n\n## 与 v1 Framing 的关键差异\n\n| 维度 | v1 | v2.2 |\n|------|----|----|\n| Headline | VOTA compression 优于 full trace | Exception-based fault detection is model-agnostic |\n| Primary claim | VOTA +24pp through fault indicator preservation | Exception signal transfers across models (gap = 0.50pp) |\n| Claim 2 | Compression > full trace (只有 gradient dilution) | Information density primary + gradient dilution quantification + cross-model reinforcement |\n| Claim 3 Tier 1 | exception_only only | exception + assertion-point (co-Tier 1, D020) |\n| Model scope | 单模型 (Qwen) | 双模型, 跨模型对比是核心贡献 |\n| VOTA 定位 | 核心方法 | Model-dependent richer representation |\n| DeepSeek full_trace | N/A | v2 killed, v3 re-run pending |\n| Seed robustness | 未验证 | ✅ 99.83% ± 0.00 (3 seeds), irreducible samples 确认 |\n"
}
